Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The mendelian variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 988 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 369 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the mendelian space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 988 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 369. QED.

Theorem 219.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a mendelian algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 146. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 253/2.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the mendelian decomposition. By minimality, the element a^146 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 253-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
